Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the ecosystems object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 327. The moduli space has dimension at most 474 with 4 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_327(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 327. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^474(S, F) is trivial. QED.
